# Non-telescopic carbon/epoxy fishing rod manufacture from supplied prepreg

All rules apply only to a complete new non-telescopic carbon-fibre/thermoset-epoxy fishing rod locally made from whole supplied prepreg with whole purchased configured guides/tip-top, reel seat, actual EVA grip and butt closure. All configured sections and joints belong to that terminal. Glass-reinforced principal structures, bamboo, telescopic or other principal forming routes, bare blanks/tubes/materials/parts, reels/line/hooks/baits/nets/tackle sets/decoys and fishing-use services are excluded. Changed principal supply or component-manufacturing boundaries require an evidence-based revision of this same CPC record before quantitative use, never a silent shortcut or parallel identity. The quantitative reference is first quality release; gross physical movements, attributed upstream burdens, inherited stocks and subsequent original-cohort service horizons are distinct dimensions. A source example, family UUID or this candidate does not establish a measured factory inventory or approval.

## 1. Scope and Applicability

This single CPC38450 record covers the manufacturing foreground of a complete new non-telescopic carbon-fibre/thermoset-epoxy fishing rod, with its actual configured sections and interfaces, installed line guides and tip-top, complete reel seat, actual EVA grip configuration and butt closure. Start with real whole externally supplied carbon/epoxy prepreg and separately scoped whole components; include receipt/refrigerated storage and actual conditioning, pattern cutting, mandrel tacking and rolling, consolidation tape, controlled curing, mandrel/tape removal, end trimming, straightness and surface grinding/sanding, actual qualified blank coating/branding, component fitting and guide wrapping, actual adhesive/coating mixing and drying/curing, checks and adjustments through unique first complete-rod quality release. Purchased-finished-rod repacking cannot satisfy this production route.

Harrison positively identifies supplier-made epoxy carbon prepreg rolls and UD/finished-blank alternatives; Richards supplies the complete blank-to-finished-rod sequence and actual assembly/coating images; Jwell independently documents carbon-prepreg-to-packed-rod operations; Fantan distinguishes locally made blanks/guides/seats from purchased components. Flex Coat supports supplied two-part wrap finish, genuine failure/correction and solvent limitations. These form a transparent compatible technical synthesis, not the BOM, measurements, factory, order or SKU of one common product. This method chooses the carbon/epoxy route and whole purchased guide/seat/grip components; no glass reinforcement, in-house guide/reel-seat manufacture, fixed section count, cure schedule, composition fraction, wall thickness, rod mass, load capacity, lifetime, certification or yield is inferred from a catalogue or process image.

Exclude reels, fishing line, hooks/baits/lures, nets, tackle sets, decoys, hunting requisites, outdoor fishing service and customer use/end-of-life. Bare prepreg, dry carbon cloth, neat resin, a hollow tube, supplied bare blank or a component alone is not the complete reference rod. Glass/composite-glass principal structures, bamboo, telescopic construction and different principal forming routes are outside this bounded method. If the actual plant buys a cured blank, makes the guide/seat/grip locally or changes another principal supply boundary, reassess and revise this same CPC record with complete evidence and disjoint inventory before quantitative use; do not silently omit upstream production or create a parallel identity. Every real auxiliary, packaging, utility, species, maintenance and treatment exchange remains required when present.

Sources: `harrison-prepreg-blank`; `richards-rod-manufacture`; `jwell-rod-factory`; `fantan-component-boundary`; `flexcoat-wrap-finish`; `un-cpc3-structure-2025`.

Declare every required qualifier in dataset metadata, process notes, reference comment, product description or equivalent data-package fields; a missing qualifier makes that data-package reference incomplete. Mass comparison does not establish equal angling performance.

Native Product family is manufactured finished fishing rods and other tackle, production mix at plant, reference Mass/kg. Here it denotes only one complete non-telescopic carbon/epoxy rod with its specified sections, joints, installed guides, tip-top, reel seat, grips and butt closure; no reel, line, hook, bait, net or use service. The isolated native comment “385 Games and toys” conflicts with the consistent rod basename and manufactured-product context; disclose this annotation defect rather than changing the physical identity. It supplies no composition, cure, stiffness, lifetime or article mass. Confirm actual complete BOM and acceptance; neither bare blank nor another tackle article is interchangeable.

| release_mass | reference product | Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` | kg | Q = sum of calibrated net complete rod masses newly first quality-released in the declared cohort. Weigh accepted whole rods with all configured sections and fitted parts, excluding detachable transport packaging and excluded fishing equipment; retain tare/calibration, moisture and configuration records. Counts use independently measured same-configuration net masses, never nominal supplier mass or a secondary native factor. |
| first_release_gate | reference product | Mass | kg | Released closing FG already belongs to Q; manufacturing burden closing stores contain only raw/WIP and genuinely unused/unassigned non-WIP burden. Delivery/retesting/returns are not new production. First-release Q is not a net-delivery denominator. |
| native_units | all inventory rows | Actual row-declared extensive property | Actual row native unit | Preserve each actual native extensive property and numerator unit. Convert measured kWh to MJ once by the exact factor 3.6. Integrated liquid/gas volumes are cumulative; integrate only actual rates. Volume-to-mass needs independently measured density of that material at its true composition/state, not water density or a secondary flow-property factor. Count Item(s) and item are equivalent by factor 1; no kg/item is inferred. |
| gross_events | gross physical cards | Actual gross-event extensive quantity V_i | Actual event native unit | For every gross card A_i = actual complete event V_i in its own unit, with no stock correction, burden-vector substitution or economic share. Its Y_i is a physical disclosure, not an additional upstream procurement or credit. The same event owner’s actual burden dimension is attributed once separately. |
| raw_collection | all collection protocols | Protocol-declared unnormalized raw extensive quantity | Actual raw-record unit | Collect original raw records without division. In protocol tables aggregation_rule labels the final per-reference view only; the raw collection method and raw_fields remain unnormalised. Prepare the applicable numerator first and execute the single_quotient rule exactly once. Amount fields and aggregation labels point to that same saved result and never divide again. |
| zero_or_unknown | all inventory rows | Mass (original cohort Q_c denominator only) `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` | kg | For Q_c = 0 preserve all real loads, stocks, failures and disposition; issue no unit result. Unknown quantities/balances/service capacity stay unresolved. Q_c is the measured original cohort reference mass for a service account, not present shipment mass or a fabricated future quantity. |

Unshipped FG has real observed service but no invented future work or completed-dispatch claim. Record actual returns, broken/held rods, attempted repairs and disposition. Manufacturer/specification permission, post-work tests and acceptance authorize continued qualified status; unauthorized, failed or rejected real work still retains all physical flows and loads. Do not presume restoration of broken composite structure or another first release. Ordinary humidity exchange needs no repair occurrence or permission. | `richards-rod-manufacture`; `flexcoat-wrap-finish`; `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |

Whole wet P/I includes supplier-applied layers, seals, ink, adhesive, fixtures and water once; supplier-included ingress remains a real physical receipt with one upstream owner. Opening/closing empty, attached WIP/FG and reusable stock are all included. Internal reuse cancels; P/I is not cancelled because its new mass remains in closing stock. W pools of one true mixed waste are independent origin subledgers, not duplicate external exchanges. Add every other actual bag/liner/thread/tube/closure separately, with one whole supplied-article bridge or disjoint site-fabrication account; no generic packaging pool exchange. | `richards-rod-manufacture`; `jwell-rod-factory`; `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| boundary_assets | support and all retained media | Keep full gross new/used asset and repair-part/flow events and actual installed oil/refrigerant/water stocks independently of service-burden allocation. Include real old parts/fluids removed and new parts/fluids received; an included new-machine charge is bridged once. Do not scale physical assets, wastes, water or gross shipments by economic shares. For material/moisture X_in/X_out remove only another incoming/outgoing flow-term duplicate on the same side, never the opposite leg or full closing stock. Retained new influx and full closing wet mass coexist. | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| boundary_utilities | each actual utility | Use actual electricity, heat, each fuel, air, every water/steam carrier and external Product return separately with full physical state/enthalpy and supplier boundary. No default boiler or steam operation. A whole utility provider is assigned once; do not add both its supply and already included own inputs, nor aggregate air plus the same gas constituents. Include real leaks, failed runs and utility treatment, with native property units retained. | `richards-rod-manufacture`; `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| boundary_species | physical mass and relevant elements | Close actual gross physical mass and relevant element ledgers before burden allocation, using independently observed species, composition and states. Whole supplied wet articles, free/carrier water, genuine reaction water and any independently evidenced structural/bound water stay distinct and non-additive. Thermoset curing does not by itself prove a water-producing reaction. Do not use one residual to fit two unknowns, assume water density, convert COD/VOC/NOx/total dust/total element tests to a chosen molecule, or fabricate matching opposite exports. | `harrison-prepreg-blank`; `richards-rod-manufacture`; `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| boundary_extend_actual | all inventory | Cards are conditional possible single exchanges, not a universal BOM or zero-filled checklist. Before a concrete data package claims completeness, instantiate every real different formulation/component/utility/species/waste/packaging/repair/treatment event as its own card with compatible identity, raw protocol and provider. Missing measurement or UUID is explicit; not_applicable needs affirmative physical-route evidence. A real mixed outlet is one Waste exchange with independently measured non-additive origins, never a way to conceal missing inputs or emissions. | `richards-rod-manufacture`; `jwell-rod-factory`; `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |

## 6. Process Inventory Structure

Each card is one actual conditional exchange and one direction. Its full description, native restrictions and numerator role govern use. All input/output masses remain genuine gross observations in cp_stocks; a burden-bearing ordinary production row may report its attributable demand while a gross card reports complete V. Each card’s collection protocol collects raw data only. Do not instantiate absent events, but retain nonzero origin W in a mixed-waste subledger even when a separate external W card is inapplicable. Additional actual exchanges require their own exact cards before a complete data-package claim.

### Process Map

| process_id | process_name | inclusion | inclusion_condition | role | quantitative_reference |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| manufacture | Prepreg-to-complete-rod manufacture | required | Actual selected route and real operations/events only | foreground | per reference flow; linked positive original Q_c for actual service |
| service | Actual post-release factory service | required | Actual selected route and real operations/events only | foreground | per reference flow; linked positive original Q_c for actual service |
| support | Actual assets and maintenance | required | Actual selected route and real operations/events only | foreground | per reference flow; linked positive original Q_c for actual service |

### Process: Prepreg-to-complete-rod manufacture (`manufacture`)

#### Inputs

##### Product flows

###### Whole supplied carbon/epoxy prepreg (`prepreg`)

Receive actual supplier-made carbon-fibre thermoset epoxy prepreg before pattern cutting; include the whole fibre/resin/additive formulation once, at its real refrigerated/conditioned supply state. Carbon tow/cloth and neat resin are not purchased again. Record every distinct actual prepreg separately, release backing and supplied protective packaging through non-overlapping inclusion bridges; no fibre/resin fraction, areal mass or cure schedule is assumed.

The exact compatible flow UUID remains unresolved; retain this one specific physical/chemical name and actual native-unit observations. No different material, state, route, whole/part, medium or type is substituted.

Prepare the true raw attributable numerator A_i for this exchange or its consumed service using the original cohort/history and actual unit conversion. Execute single_quotient exactly once against positive measured Q_c and save Y_i. The Amount field and aggregation label identify that same result; they do not divide a previously divided amount. Preserve full physical gross V separately, even if new material remains in closing stock.

- Specificity: Site-specific (`site_specific`)
- Normalization basis: per reference flow

###### Polypropylene consolidation film tape (`compaction_film`)

Use actual supplied film strip during mandrel winding/tape compaction; weigh full issues, retained unused strip and stripped waste. This is a forming consumable, not finished-rod packaging. Its actual supplier must establish the polymer and operating compatibility; another tape construction needs its specific actual card.

Native Product is plastic film, Mass/kg, whose definition includes PVC, PE, PP, PS and other resin films; no fixed grade, route or geography is declared. This card selects one actual polypropylene film tape used for consolidation, only when the supplier identifies this film and its true heat/shrink/release compatibility. It is not TPT/PET laminate, resin granulate, adhesive packing tape, fabric or an arbitrary mixture. No thickness, shrink force, cure temperature or film-to-mass factor follows from the family name.

Prepare the true raw attributable numerator A_i for this exchange or its consumed service using the original cohort/history and actual unit conversion. Execute single_quotient exactly once against positive measured Q_c and save Y_i. The Amount field and aggregation label identify that same result; they do not divide a previously divided amount. Preserve full physical gross V separately, even if new material remains in closing stock.

##### Elementary flows

###### Molecular oxygen natural intake (`service_o2in`)

Only actual natural intake to an on-site operation within this gate. Resolve real composition/flow/mass and destination from observations; preserve unused pass-through and any retained fraction. Purchased compressed air is not a second natural-air supply at the receiving process.

Keep this measured energy in its own cohort and observed horizon. An outsourced operation carries its compatible upstream provider rather than a second on-site meter charge.

Native Product is CN grid-average consumption-mix alternating current delivered to the user below 1 kV; reference Net calorific value/MJ. Use this UUID only for that actual country, voltage and delivery mix. Other countries, generation-specific power or another voltage require a compatible identity/provider. Meter kWh and convert once with the exact 3.6 MJ/kWh relation. Site compressor/oven electricity is not purchased again through an overlapping heat/air provider.

###### Actual reusable hydraulic fluid returned outside (`hydraulic_return`)

Only actual outgoing reusable Product fluid with full volume/mass/state and no netting against new additions or installed closing charge. A waste oil outlet is separate. No avoided-lubricant credit.

Native Product is whole engineered hydraulic fluid, mineral or synthetic base with performance additives, reference Volume/m3. Use only the actual hydraulic medium and supplied preparation. Declare grade, base, additives, water, measured density and state; oil volume is not water mass. New supply, installed charge, removed used fluid, leakage and external return are disjoint events; included initial machine charge is not procured again.

Any real attributed burden for the event has one separate owner/dimension.

## 7. Allocation and Co-product Handling

Only the following attributional allocation conditions apply; physical gross quantities remain outside burden shares.

| rule_id | Applies to | Rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| allocation_scope | only attributional cases requiring allocation | Apply this hierarchy only in the declared attributional modelling situation where allocation is required. Manufacturer process text does not establish allocation. Separate exclusive operations and exchange burdens first, by actual subdivision or defensible virtual subdivision; raw materials embodied solely in the rod and its specific local operations remain with that function. | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| allocation_physical | remaining individual exchanges | For each remaining exchange identify the determining physical causal relationship and real measured driver to each co-function; justify why that driver changes that exchange. Different exchanges may need different causes. Actual machine/cure cycles, time with the relevant capacity/occupied volume/area, coated surface or measured material pathway may support a choice, but mass, item count, processing time and economics are not freely interchangeable defaults. Assigned vectors across functions must sum to the original vector. | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| allocation_residual | genuinely unresolved joint-process remainder | Only after documenting why subdivision and determining physical causality cannot resolve the remaining joint-process burden may contemporaneous market values at the same real common process gate and supplied quantity/quality/state allocate that remainder. Remove downstream transport/conditioning margins consistently when deriving gate values; do not compare retail finished rods with unconditioned waste or apply a fixed economic ratio to all flows. | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| allocation_waste | failures samples scraps and treatment | Ordinary production waste, rejection, breakage, destructive samples and their handling/treatment stay with the production/service function causing them, even when externally transferred or sold. A one-time burden transfer T_other requires an actual distinct other cohort/function and justified allocation/handover, with destination, state and complete inherited vector; sale or external shipment alone is insufficient. Full gross physical legs never shrink. No automatic avoided virgin material, energy, heat or disposal credit. | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| allocation_stocks | stocks assets packaging and service | Attribute only actual consumed inherited burden to the correct cohort. Manufacturing uses B_open + U - B_close - justified T_other, where B_close excludes already first-released FG and includes only genuine raw/WIP/unassigned stores. Original-cohort performed service remains attributable through its observed H even for held FG; only unused/unassigned charge or capacity burden is deferred. Asset physical acquisitions and package gross events retain V in full, while their upstream/service burden is owned and assigned once separately. A stock-corrected source record cannot be corrected again. | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |

## 8. Foreground Data Collection, Calculation, and Quality Rules

Collection tables preserve raw non-divided data. aggregation_rule names only the final per-reference view and repeats no arithmetic. The sole quotient is single_quotient; saved Y_i on a card identifies that exact result. The complete description and numerator role govern what A_i means, including A_i=V_i for every gross card.

### Data Collection Protocols

| protocol_id | process_id | flow_role | record_type | raw_fields | collection_method | unit | frequency | temporal_coverage | site_scope | aggregation_rule | quality_evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| cp_release | manufacture | Unique complete-rod first release | acceptance_record | serial; model; configuration; complete_BOM; actual_section_count; net_rod_mass_kg; moisture_state; tare; calibration; inspection_tests; unique_first_release_decision; cohort_Q; opening_FG; closing_released_FG; return_history | Weigh accepted complete actual configured rods on a calibrated scale excluding detachable transport packaging and excluded fishing equipment; reconcile all sections and installed components with the controlled BOM and unique first-release decision. Sum real net released masses once to Q. If counts are used, independently weigh matching actual configuration samples and retain sampling uncertainty; no catalogue mass. Collection remains raw and undivided. | kg | Every actual first-release decision | Complete production cohort | Named manufacturing release gate | per reference flow | Scale/tare/calibration; full BOM; original acceptance and serial history |
| cp_stocks | manufacture | Disjoint physical stocks and water/species | stock_record | actual_process_gate; raw_WIP_nonWIP_asset_FG_pools; opening; full_wet_closing; composition; completion; actual_gross_in_out; internal_handoff_pairs; P_I_ER_S_EO_W; reaction_evidence; X_in_out; actual_species; moisture_phase; source_cohort | Measure complete physical opening/closing pools and every real incoming/outgoing event independently; keep retained incoming material/water together with full closing wet stock. ### Calculation Rules

| rule_id | Applies to | Formula or rule | Inputs | Output | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| raw_unit_conversion | raw observations before attribution | Convert only true original units: MJ = 3.6 * measured_kWh; kg_i = cumulative_volume_i * independently_measured_density_i_at_actual_state. Integrate a genuine rate once over its matched observed time, whereas a cumulative meter/integrated volume is not multiplied by time. Keep carrier mass/enthalpy and relevant constituent mass disjoint from their whole supply owner. | raw_observation; observed_time; measured_density; actual_state | converted_raw | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| cohort_numerators | burden dimensions only | For each original exchange dimension retain the full inherited vectors. Manufacturing attributable A_i = B_open_i + U_i - B_close_i - justified_T_other_i, with B_close limited to raw/WIP/genuinely unused or unassigned non-WIP stores. Use an already stock-corrected U directly without subtracting stores again. For original-cohort service A_i contains all actual consumed/performed burden through H plus inherited assigned burden; defer only genuinely unused/unassigned capacity/charge burden. Released FG is not B_close. Ordinary waste/sale is not T_other. These burden dimensions are not the physical numerators of gross cards. | B_open_i; U_i; B_close_i; justified_T_other_i; cohort; H; cp_vectors | attributed_burden_A_i | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| gross_numerators | every gross physical card | A_i = V_i, the full actual independently measured gross physical event in that card’s own native unit. Never substitute B_open + U - B_close, service burden, a coproduct/economic share or net shipments. Its disclosed physical Y_i creates no extra procurement/provider/credit. The same real event’s upstream or service burden is owned and attributed once as a distinct burden dimension. | V_i; event_id; physical_state; owner; cp_vectors | physical_A_i | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| pack_pool_balance | each actual single packaging pool | Opening_empty + Opening_attached_WIP_FG + Opening_reusable + P + I + ER + actual_disjoint_repair_or_moisture_in = Closing_empty + Closing_attached_WIP_FG + Closing_reusable + gross_S + EO + W_raw + actual_disjoint_moisture_or_material_out. Internal reuse/handoffs cancel once. Whole wet P/I and their water/components are not added twice. X_in/X_out remove only overlapping other incoming/outgoing flow terms on the same side, never a stock or the opposite physical leg; new retained influx and complete closing wet mass both remain. For one mixed outlet sum non-overlapping origin W_raw only to the measured whole mixture and assign its treatment/provider once. | opening_pools; closing_pools; P; I; ER; gross_S; EO; W_raw; disjoint_bridges; cp_vectors | pack_physical_closure | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| physical_gate_balance | manufacturing/FG/consolidated physical gates | For each disjoint physical material and relevant element use actual opening mass + actual gross incoming + independently evidenced reaction/source terms = actual closing mass + actual gross outgoing. Manufacture includes Q as its internal first-release handoff; FG includes the matching Q receipt; consolidation cancels them once. Keep water absorbed/evaporated in ordinary storage, carrier returns, retained installed fluids, failures and actual losses. Reaction/free/phase/structural water need separate independent evidence; one fitted residual cannot establish multiple unknowns. This physical equation is never scaled by burden allocation shares. | opening_physical; gross_in; reaction_evidence; closing_physical; gross_out; Q; cp_stocks | physical_and_element_closure | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| single_quotient | all inventory rows | Y_i = A_i / Q_c. A_i is the prepared raw numerator of the selected dimension; gross cards use complete V_i, burden dimensions use only their justified attributable vector, and the reference row uses its own Q. Q_c is positive measured first-release cohort mass or the linked original cohort mass for actual service. Execute once and store Y_i; card Amount fields and protocol aggregation labels refer to this same saved result without another division. With Q_c = 0 retain raw loads/stocks and issue no unit result. | A_i; Q_c; cp_release; cp_vectors | Y_i | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |

### Data Quality Requirements

| requirement_id | Applies to | Requirement | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| dq_complete_identity | reference and supplied articles | Match the actual complete rod BOM, supply state and every hard native material/form/phase/route/grade/geography/media/type restriction; a UUID or catalogue weight alone is insufficient. Keep unresolved ordinary auxiliary identities explicit. | Supplier technical records; original native identity; controlled drawings; incoming/first-release inspection |
| dq_measurement | all raw observations | Retain original non-divided measurements with units, uncertainty, calibration, tare, density/state and rate/integral distinction; no nominal rod mass or composition inferred from a conversion coefficient. | Raw scale/meter records; calibration; material-state tests; calculation trail |
| dq_cohort | cohorts and stores | Account every failure, zero-output period, start/stop, cleanup, test, rejected and attempted rework load with opening/closing raw/WIP/non-WIP physical and every inherited exchange vector. No second stock correction or released-FG manufacture deduction. | Lot/serial history; stock-state ledgers; complete source-vector provenance |
| dq_services | actual original-cohort service | Identify original Q_c, observed H and actual performed work independently of FG dispatch. Continued qualification requires permission/spec and post-work tests/acceptance; performed unauthorized/failed work remains recorded with real hold/waste disposition. | Service-time/driver records; manufacturer decisions; tests; disposition register |
| dq_pools | packaging repair and assets | Verify each actual packaging six-leg pool and full wet stocks, whole supplied-article and disjoint repair/moisture bridges, new/used asset/part events, installed fluid and consumed capacity burden. Check every card description for wrong inherited identities; pallet is not plank, PE not PET, film not resin, whole guide not a ring alone. | Matched gross event IDs; measured component origins; physical balances; upstream owner links |
| dq_allocation | burden attribution | Document subdivision, each determining physical cause and any genuinely residual same-gate contemporaneous market values; prove full vector conservation. Waste sale/external transfer does not itself justify T_other or a credit. | Causal driver evidence; common-gate states/prices; receiver handover; conserved vectors |
| dq_sources | external technical evidence | Use complete relevant originals and context; distinguish product-route examples from measured plant data and allocation methodology. No supplier’s numeric recipe or operating example becomes a universal default. | Primary technical originals; source-specific applicability and limitations; complete context |
| dq_completeness | concrete foreground data package | Instantiate all actual exchanges, provider boundaries and real species/outlets before claiming completeness; disclose missing quantities/providers/composition and uncertainty. Ordinary gaps do not prove absent manufacture, and failed retrieval does not prove absence in a database. | Complete actual BOM/route/meter/outlet reconciliation and unresolved-gap register |

## 9. Validation Rules

| rule_id | Applies to | Rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| validate_terminal | complete reference rod | Reject a bare blank/material/part or different tackle terminal. Check every required qualifier, whole-rod BOM, same configured first-release Q and explicit finished_rod Product output. A compatible broad native family does not approve another terminal or methodology. | `richards-rod-manufacture`; `jwell-rod-factory`; `fantan-component-boundary` |
| validate_measurement | all inventory rows | Require native unit chains and actual numerator measurements/conversions, the correct original Q_c/H, one single_quotient application and no per-card or aggregation redivision. Unknown relationships remain inconclusive. Q_c zero retains raw burden and no normalized output; check results are not measured factory data. | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| validate_physical | physical gates and gross cards | Independently reconcile manufacture, FG and consolidated full physical/relevant-element gates; cancel internal Q/handoffs once. Gross V is never an attributed burden or economic share. Check all packaging P/I/ER/S/EO/W events, true full wet opening/closing stores, retained new influx, every same-side X bridge, one actual mixed-waste outlet and provider/treatment once. No invented symmetric outlets or residual-fitted species. | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| validate_failures | production and service history | Preserve every actual failed/rejected/unauthorized attempt, waste, sample, treatment, startup, idle and rework burden, including Q-zero cohorts. Manufacturer permission and actual post-work acceptance control continued qualification, not deletion of already performed work. No broken-composite restoration is presumed. | `richards-rod-manufacture`; `flexcoat-wrap-finish`; `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| validate_supply | each selected flow card | Check actual native material/form/grade/route/geography/phase/media/type and complete property restrictions in each description; do not reuse a Product UUID for a Waste outlet, a whole-asset/pallet description for a repair part, a recycling-plant state for contaminated factory waste or a total indicator as a molecule. Missing compatible auxiliary UUIDs/providers remain explicit and cannot justify a fabricated binding. | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| validate_complete_context | allmethodconditions | Retain all complete applicability, native-admission, collection, first-release, physical/provider and analytical conditions below in both language projections; no ID-only match, finite pass or future SOP supplies missing scientific evidence. | `harrison-prepreg-blank`; `richards-rod-manufacture`; `jwell-rod-factory`; `fantan-component-boundary`; `flexcoat-wrap-finish`; `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010`; `un-cpc3-structure-2025`; `epa-method320`; `epa-method3c`; `epa-method5` |

### Complete applicability and accounting context

This single CPC38450 record covers the manufacturing foreground of a complete new non-telescopic carbon-fibre/thermoset-epoxy fishing rod, with its actual configured sections and interfaces, installed line guides and tip-top, complete reel seat, actual EVA grip configuration and butt closure. Start with real whole externally supplied carbon/epoxy prepreg and separately scoped whole components; include receipt/refrigerated storage and actual conditioning, pattern cutting, mandrel tacking and rolling, consolidation tape, controlled curing, mandrel/tape removal, end trimming, straightness and surface grinding/sanding, actual qualified blank coating/branding, component fitting and guide wrapping, actual adhesive/coating mixing and drying/curing, checks and adjustments through unique first complete-rod quality release. Purchased-finished-rod repacking cannot satisfy this production route.

Harrison positively identifies supplier-made epoxy carbon prepreg rolls and UD/finished-blank alternatives; Richards supplies the complete blank-to-finished-rod sequence and actual assembly/coating images; Jwell independently documents carbon-prepreg-to-packed-rod operations; Fantan distinguishes locally made blanks/guides/seats from purchased components. Flex Coat supports supplied two-part wrap finish, genuine failure/correction and solvent limitations. These form a transparent compatible technical synthesis, not the BOM, measurements, factory, order or SKU of one common product. This method chooses the carbon/epoxy route and whole purchased guide/seat/grip components; no glass reinforcement, in-house guide/reel-seat manufacture, fixed section count, cure schedule, composition fraction, wall thickness, rod mass, load capacity, lifetime, certification or yield is inferred from a catalogue or process image.

Exclude reels, fishing line, hooks/baits/lures, nets, tackle sets, decoys, hunting requisites, outdoor fishing service and customer use/end-of-life. Bare prepreg, dry carbon cloth, neat resin, a hollow tube, supplied bare blank or a component alone is not the complete reference rod. Glass/composite-glass principal structures, bamboo, telescopic construction and different principal forming routes are outside this bounded method. If the actual plant buys a cured blank, makes the guide/seat/grip locally or changes another principal supply boundary, reassess and revise this same CPC record with complete evidence and disjoint inventory before quantitative use; do not silently omit upstream production or create a parallel identity. Every real auxiliary, packaging, utility, species, maintenance and treatment exchange remains required when present.

Declare every required qualifier in dataset metadata, process notes, reference comment, product description or equivalent data-package fields; a missing qualifier makes that data-package reference incomplete. Mass comparison does not establish equal angling performance.

Native Product family is manufactured finished fishing rods and other tackle, production mix at plant, reference Mass/kg. Here it denotes only one complete non-telescopic carbon/epoxy rod with its specified sections, joints, installed guides, tip-top, reel seat, grips and butt closure; no reel, line, hook, bait, net or use service. The isolated native comment “385 Games and toys” conflicts with the consistent rod basename and manufactured-product context; disclose this annotation defect rather than changing the physical identity. It supplies no composition, cure, stiffness, lifetime or article mass. Confirm actual complete BOM and acceptance; neither bare blank nor another tackle article is interchangeable.

Each card is one actual conditional exchange and one direction. Its full description, native restrictions and numerator role govern use. All input/output masses remain genuine gross observations in cp_stocks; a burden-bearing ordinary production row may report its attributable demand while a gross card reports complete V. Each card’s collection protocol collects raw data only. Do not instantiate absent events, but retain nonzero origin W in a mixed-waste subledger even when a separate external W card is inapplicable. Additional actual exchanges require their own exact cards before a complete data-package claim.

Collection tables preserve raw non-divided data. aggregation_rule names only the final per-reference view and repeats no arithmetic. The sole quotient is single_quotient; saved Y_i on a card identifies that exact result. The complete description and numerator role govern what A_i means, including A_i=V_i for every gross card.

Lock Q0 to unique original complete-rod first-quality-release events at the fixed completed-manufacturing gate. On that original mass/configuration/moisture basis, partition each cohort across disjoint net remaining deliveries, every actual on-site released state, irreversible losses and other terminal dispositions. Gross returns, repacking, retests, attempted repair and rework create no new event. Delivery plus closing FG is sufficient only when no other postgate states or exits exist. Changes in moisture, removed/replaced parts or finish require independently measured component/state conversions. Do not restore lost mass to a surviving rod and count that same loss again. Original Q0 remains fixed for all later services, and Q0=0/unknown retains raw totals with unresolved normalization.

Keep the complete raw/WIP/FG, installed/spare/retired assets, packaging and retained-fluid physical ledger independently from the acceptance-event ledger and every attributable environmental/provider-service vector. Own opening WIP inherits its actual complete earlier foreground burdens once; closing WIP carries substantiated burdens to future cohorts. A provider aggregate and decomposition of its already embedded manufacturing/transport/asset history are mutually exclusive. Include actual failed, zero-output, cleanup, start/stop and rejected work. Neither already released FG nor an allocation share changes physical stocks or removes an already incurred burden.

For the physical display only, Qanalysis denotes the declared positive original Q_c=Q0, not a new acceptance basis. Each genuine contaminated wipe, glove, abrasive, stripped film, discarded package, equipment or mixed waste is one whole actual recipient event Qt containing its substrate, retained resin/solvent/dust and water once. Dry substrate and retained constituents are nested analytical/origin ledgers; they are not extra exported wastes unless physically separated before the boundary. Preserve full receiver/provider Qt and its actual extensive-unit basis. The gross-card saved Y=Qt/Qanalysis is a physical disclosure and never automatically a cohort treatment/provider demand. Model separate evidenced nonoverlapping treatment demand A/Q0 in compatible provider units with original cohort genealogy; do not shrink physical Qt by burden shares or charge full Qt to every served cohort. Unknown service shares/original Q0 remain unresolved. A separately collected stream and its fraction in a real mixed outlet cannot both be exports.

Arithmetic example only, not a source or study default: one10kg contaminated wipe event contains8kg substrate plus2kg retained resin/solvent/water. Independently evidenced25%/75% compatible treatment-service assignment gives2.5/7.5kg-equivalent demand; original Q0 of100/200kg gives0.025/0.0375kg-equivalent per reference. The physical receiver gets10kg once; there is no2.5kg physical waste, extra2kg contaminant export, duplicated10kg service demand or assumed sharing/lifetime.

Maintain actual complete prepreg production upstream and actual local cut/roll/tape/cure/strip/trim/grind/finish/assembly. A whole supplied prepreg system includes its actual fibre, formulated resin, additives, supplied moisture, conditioning and transport once. The real additional resin/prepainting operation mentioned in the Richards route must be resolved against the actual supplied system: it is a separate specific whole actual formulation only if separately added, not silently omitted or bought again if embedded. A supplied finished blank cannot replace the manufacturing scope; a genuinely separate upstream blank, gas-control, thermal, steam, compressed-air, wastewater or utility producer must have complete actual matched inputs, composition, outputs, reaction chemistry, energy and waste burdens. Moving a known on-site unit to an assumed purchase/receiving stub is not allowed. Add every actual reagent, carrier, fuel, part and outlet as a specific atomic interface.

Actual alcohol flames, other real combustion and any thermal control require complete actual fuel/preparation, air or independently measured O2/N2/Ar/water intake, reactive species generation/consumption, final gas species, heat and losses; CO2 alone is not a burner. No source mentions a universal boiler, natural-gas grade, propane torch use or complete combustion for every rod. Preserve actual water supplied inside whole formulations, separately added/dilution water, steam/condensate/returns, absorbed moisture, evaporation and independently evidenced reaction water without double counting. Thermoset curing does not itself prove water formation or a fixed emission fraction. Internal transfers cancel once while real production/control/utility burdens remain.

Every candidate card is conditional on a real present interface; record present, evidenced absent, below detection and unknown distinctly. A source manufacturing example is not a measured current factory inventory. Actual whole formulations remain one supplied preparation only with full known supply/BOM/provider boundaries; generic VOC/NOx/COD/dust indicators cannot invent chemical species or fixed constituent fractions. Identify actual final release species, phase, medium and sampling position after real controls; collected whole condensate or mixed effluent goes to its actual receiver instead of also becoming the same emitted mass. Any necessary analytical/matrix/phase procedure not established remains a core methodological hold; future SOP, calibration or finite-check success cannot waive it. Ordinary actual site quantities, calibrations and provider qualification remain explicit study obligations, not invented defaults or a requirement for the same source factory/SKU.

Actual environmental analysis uses procedures within their real matrix and phase scope. EPA Method320 supplies extractive FTIR for IR-active vapor analytes with reference spectra, interference assessment, complete heated/validated conditioning, analyte-spike transport/recovery and its explicit matrix-validation procedure; it does not measure homonuclear N2/O2 or monatomic Ar or every particulate/liquid phase. EPA Method3C supplies GC-TCD component analysis, calibration/linearity, repeat analysis and wet/dry/dilution corrections for CO2/CH4/N2/O2 within its stated landfill/otherwise specified source scope; it is not automatic authorization for this rod plant or Ar analysis. Retain its collection cross-references and actual sampling/air-ingress/condensate-loss qualification; do not subtract real carrier or combustion N2/O2 as assumed contamination. EPA Method5 supplies its defined filterable-PM isokinetic sampling, recovery and gravimetry, not all condensable or wet-mist mass. Cured composite sanding PM is the actual whole particulate mixture, with independently evidenced composition, not pure elemental carbon or a default split. Actual Ar and any further genuine species require their applicable actual species/matrix procedure; no default atmospheric fraction or residual-fitted Ar is allowed. Collected mist/condensate and genuine liquid effluent have their own whole receiver and actual composition. An unestablished essential procedure remains an explicit hold; site calibration/QA within an established procedure is a separate uncompleted study qualification, never claimed passed here.


Agilent5994-4552EN (18 January2022), Noble Gases Analysis Using the Agilent990 Micro GC, demonstrates Ar/O2 baseline separation in a nitrogen-balance standard and nitrogen/air analysis using a Molesieve5Å micro-GC channel, helium carrier and moisture/CO2 backflush protection. Its single-concentration repeatability and detection-limit results establish an analytical approach, not multipoint calibration/linearity, complete sample-train recovery or applicability to this rod plant’s actual hot/wet final outlet. Column protection does not prove quantitative recovery through conditioning. For each genuine Ar leg retain the actual intake or final outlet, sample time and flow, wet/dry basis, temperature/pressure, background and air ingress, conditioning and condensate losses, analyte-specific response/calibration, Ar/O2 separation and actual matrix/transport recovery evidence before integration to kg. Do not transfer source settings, concentrations, detection limits, repeatability thresholds or atmospheric fractions as defaults; do not infer Ar from a residual. An unestablished essential sampling/analytical/matrix procedure remains hold; ordinary calibration and QA within an established procedure remain separate actual-study obligations, not a claim of qualification. This source does not establish any actual plant emission or repair another unknown species/phase method.

## 10. Published Dataset Profile

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| dataset_role | manufacturing_foreground |
| downstream_use | secondary_dataset; background_dataset |
| allowed_use | The exact declared complete rod configuration and evidenced supply/manufacturing route after actual foreground collection and explicit gap disclosure |
| excluded_use | All CPC38450 products by default; other terminals/routes; universal per-rod weight/performance/lifetime; fishing service; complete cradle-to-gate claims without upstream links |
| required_metadata | actual site and reporting period; complete configured rod BOM and revision; non-telescopic carbon thermoset-epoxy structure and actual section/joint configuration; whole prepreg supplied state and supplier fibre/resin/finish boundary; whole guide and tip-top frames/rings supplied once; complete reel-seat hardware and no reel/line/hooks/baits supplied; actual EVA grip and butt closure inclusions; actual cutting/rolling/tape/cure/trim/sanding/coating/branding/assembly conditions; actual whole adhesive/coating/cleaning formulations and disjoint mixing subaccounts; manufacturer-required whole-rod inspections and acceptance/disposition; calibrated complete net rod mass and actual moisture/configuration; unique first-release cohort Q and release history; raw/WIP/non-WIP stores and complete per-exchange inherited vectors; released FG history and original service Q_c/H without new Q; full gross packaging P/I/ER/S/EO/W pools and upstream owner; actual assets/new and used repair parts/installed fluid and moisture bridges; actual utility supplier geography/voltage/delivery carrier and enthalpy; real molecular gas phases/sources/receiving media and actual waste outlets; complete native family restrictions in each selected card; unresolved identities/providers/quantities and uncertainty disclosed |
| required_quality_disclosure | Actual raw measurements and uncertainty; every unresolved flow/provider/quantity; stock/vector and physical closure; observed service horizon; exact native applicability; no implied review or publication |
| update_trigger | Actual rod structure/supply-form/route/native identity or supplier boundary changes; evidence or test criteria change; new actual flows or unresolved closure |
